# QA Run Report — 2026-10-02 — Remaining untested journeys

- **Scope:** Every scenario marked `untested` at the start of this cycle: 216 scenarios across 70 existing journeys, plus three missing journey links repaired during planning (71 journeys after assigning scheduler drain). Run every feasible public-interface leg, repair reproduced product bugs, re-walk fixes, and deliver a detailed PR with current-head CI.
- **Cadence tier:** full backlog sweep; scenario counts account for scope, while persona journey debriefs establish coverage.
- **Build:** base `f0c134ad7` (v0.3.0), branch `fix/qa-untested-20261002`. Build changes are recorded with each repair.
- **Environment:** isolated macOS lab, real daemon/CLI and Web; native providers retain operator authentication according to their home policy. External prerequisites are evaluated per session, never assumed unavailable.
- **Current accounting:** 43/218 terminal (7 Pass, 33 Fixed, 3 Blocked: spoken VoiceOver, paired remote Gateway, and native notification delivery); 175 Pending.
- **Started:** 2026-10-02T22:05:40Z · **Status:** in-progress
- **Manifest:** `/Users/pedronauck/dev/qa-labs/compozy-untested-20261002-20261002-220540-224425-lab/qa-artifacts/qa/bootstrap-manifest.json`
- **Runtime home:** `/var/folders/7x/xg204hnd04b81fczcxvjlhzr0000gn/T/compozyqa-cc3f6090eb9e/runtime`
- **Base URL:** `http://127.0.0.1:50727`
- **Baseline:** `docs/qa/evidence/2026-10-02-untested/baseline.json`; no prior pass or blocked result is silently treated as new evidence.
- **Preservation:** original `fix-skills` checkout and all unrelated daemons/builds remain untouched.

- **Empty-onboarding lab:** `/Users/pedronauck/dev/qa-labs/compozy-onboarding-empty-20261002-20261002-225212-546348-lab/qa-artifacts/qa/bootstrap-manifest.json` (targeted Web/CLI/API; dedicated empty state, daemon port 54520).
- **Profile-recovery lab:** `/Users/pedronauck/dev/qa-labs/compozy-profile-recovery-20261003-20261003-140603-328060-lab/qa-artifacts/qa/bootstrap-manifest.json` (targeted CLI/API/runtime/Web; daemon 55651, separate runtime home and registered PID; source fe8a644b1). ## Session Debriefs

### CH-daemon-schema-parity — Ada

- **Ran:** 2026-10-02T22:10Z → 22:17Z (30-minute box respected).
- **Observed:** Fresh real daemon status agrees across HTTP, UDS, and CLI, including workspace-scoped reads. Bounded identity responses are identical. Global v125 and memory v3 migration streams, counts, and digests survive an explicit CLI stop and restart; the PID changes. The immutable charter's historical v1 example is superseded by the current scenario's two-stream contract, not imposed on an upgraded product.
- **Scenarios settled:** RT-001 → pass; historical fix retained, fresh retest passed.
- **Evidence:** `docs/qa/evidence/2026-10-02-untested/daemon-workspace-observations.json` indexes exact command/output receipts. Comparisons remove only generated/probe timestamps, not product fields.
- **Findings / paper cuts:** none in this scope. No direct store reads or mock services.
- **Next:** session-store ownership requires independent session data and remains Pending.

### CH-untested-valid-025-operate-daemon-schema-dora — Dora

- **Ran:** 2026-10-02T22:12:17.671753+00:00 → 2026-10-02T22:16:14.646232+00:00 (recorded action timestamps).
- **Observed:** Doctor HTTP/UDS/CLI payloads agree apart from generated time and measured duration. All 37 items carry evidence; severity counts exactly match items. `only=runtime.memory`, exclusion, quiet filtering, and an unknown filter remain structured. The log-tail diagnostic carries `status=available`. The memory diagnostic remains readable after restart.
- **Scenarios settled:** RT-002 → pass; previous log-tail repair reverified.
- **Runtime errors:** unavailable optional provider binaries and unbound forge/Tailscale credentials are accurately diagnosed. They are prerequisites for later affected journeys, not a failure of Doctor.
- **Evidence:** same indexed receipt set; `doctor-*.json` retain all outcomes.
- **Findings / paper cuts:** no new product bug. Optional providers are not silently disabled to make diagnostics green.

### CH-workspace-registration-recovery — Bruno

- **Ran:** 2026-10-02T22:13:36.427436+00:00 → 2026-10-02T22:16:14.810042+00:00 (recorded action timestamps).
- **Observed:** Registered Studio through HTTP and Editorial through UDS and independently read both. Relative roots/additional paths and empty names return 400; duplicate names/roots and conflicting rename return 409. Valid name/additional-directory updates are unchanged after the rejected writes and daemon restart. A default-agent update through UDS is independently visible through HTTP.
- **Scenarios settled:** RT-005, RT-007 → pass.
- **Abandonment/recovery:** returned to a refused update with valid values and retained the same workspace identity after restarting the daemon.
- **Evidence:** same indexed receipt set; `workspace-*.json` preserve requests and responses.
- **Findings / paper cuts:** none. Only owned lab directories were registered.

### CH-untested-066-operate-daemon-schema-ada — Ada (partial walk)

- **Ran:** 2026-10-02T22:13:36.255771+00:00 → 2026-10-02T22:14:44.853793+00:00 (recorded action timestamps).
- **Observed:** Raw overview JSON matches HTTP/UDS after removing generated/observed times. JSONL emits metadata plus all seven sections, human output renders the six promised headings, workspace selection works, windows 7/90 succeed, and 8 refuses (API 422; CLI exit 1).
- **Still required:** populate attention with a real actionable object and compare its offered actions with accepted public verbs. Empty attention is not evidence for that promise.
- **Scenarios settled:** none; RT-observe-overview-cli remains untested.
- **Evidence:** `overview-*.json` in the same indexed set.


### CH-profile-lifecycle-plan-recovery — Ada (partial walk)

- **Ran:** 2026-10-02T22:20:21.173140+00:00 → 2026-10-02T22:26:56.012932+00:00 (recorded action timestamps).
- **Observed:** Profile identity validation refuses a capitalized non-catalog icon; lowercase `briefcase` and a single emoji create durable profiles. `default` refuses archive. Workspace remembrance, Global remembrance, environment override, and explicit flag resolve in order. Two promptless sessions retain different profile IDs; session-bound native `profile_current` returns each owner through the public tool-invoke surface. HTTP and UDS independently read those same owners.

- Trigger preview submission errors remain visible beside the persistent actions
  (BUG-20260713-workspace-trigger-loop-submit-inert, regression).
- Loop mapping examples use the accepted template expression
  (BUG-20261003-loop-mapping-example-rejected).
- Webhook preview samples serialize strings as valid JSON
  (BUG-20261003-webhook-sample-invalid-json).
- Failed Trigger detail reads offer the existing catalog-return action
  (BUG-20261003-trigger-error-hides-return).
  These four bounded Web repairs have owning-suite and fresh Bruno replay evidence below;
  their local delivery commit is recorded after the required gate.


Profile rename now selects the repository offers returned by the current plan and preserves
explicit declines while editing or reviewing a refreshed plan. E2E-016 and a fresh Sol replay
verify selected-only folder movement, cancellation, HTTP409 recovery, original profile identity
and persistence. BUG-20261003-profile-rename-repositories-unchecked is verified; full lifecycle
coverage remains in progress.

Native workspace input now uses the registration key before policy and dispatch. Fresh replay
recovers the existing disabled job through every supported workspace selector and independently
through the direct automation CLI. A new named-workspace job survives persistence and is then
removed through the public interface. A fresh hosted approve-reads session reads Studio by name
without an own-project prompt, while two Editorial requests require approval and are denied after
Ada rejects them. Editorial remains unchanged. BUG-20261002-native-workspace-identity-boundaries
is verified; `native-identity-replay-*` and the owning race/integration logs retain the receipts.
The separate native-hook dispatch failure still keeps row 6 Pending.

Native operator approvals now bind workspace references before calculating their effective digest.
Fresh replay accepts names, paths and inherited workspace input, preserves supplied/digest-only
approvals, rejects changed destinations and token reuse, and crosses HTTP/UDS in both directions.
The boot composition regression fails before the repair, then the native/approval race suites and
all affected local gate lanes pass. Evidence: `approval-input-*.log`, `approval-fixed-*.json` and
BUG-20261002-native-approval-input-mismatch (fix `bef9a13b8`). The additional hook fixture exposed
stale public `tool_name` guidance. BUG-20261002-hook-tool-matcher-docs updates the reference to
the existing `tool_id` contract, verified through create/restart/catalog/delete and cleanup. The
scoped operator invocation did not fire the hook, so no hook rewrite verdict is claimed. Both fresh
replay sessions are stopped. Row 6 remains Pending for real hosted-agent boundaries.

The situation service now reads the bound session model before the configured default. The same
projection feeds authenticated context reads and fresh prompt augmentation, preserving pending
selection semantics and the existing wire shape. RT-031's fresh provider replay confirms the repair.

The shared redactor now preserves public credential requirement metadata and discovery schemas. Both regression cases failed before the repair; the complete race-enabled suite passes with 80.1% coverage. The rebuilt CLI returns a structured tool-info result identical to HTTP and UDS and successfully invokes the discovered profile-list tool. The completed continuation also proves cursor/hash/tool-ID reuse, live synthetic-secret containment, cross-workspace refusal, and cleanup; fix `196cd3010`. Evidence: `redaction-fixed-*.json` and `redact-race.log` in this cycle's evidence directory.

Planning linked RT-005 and RT-007 to workspace registration/recovery in J-operate-workspace-context and TA-048 to J-drain-scheduler. New charters cover those previously unplanned legs.

The session-list formatter now restores the documented profile-resolution prefix in JSONL. Empty and populated scoped regressions failed first, then passed with the fix; the complete CLI race suite passed in 12.102s. Rebuilt CLI reads confirm one leading frame and trailing pagination for scoped and aggregate queries. Selection fallback and machine-command parity also passed in the later replay; fix commit `42db6579f`, affected local gate green. PR delivery remains in progress. The test-shape checker has 13 unchanged legacy findings elsewhere in `session_test.go` (base: 14); both touched tests use canonical subtests.

The profile selection API now carries optional fallback provenance without changing its effective profile. A rebuilt daemon verifies the complete archive/unarchive path through both transports and the CLI, including the JSONL prefix. Complete API-core and CLI race suites pass. Onboarding's two grid sections now shrink within their columns, preserving long-path truncation and access to Skip; four-width browser replay confirms the repair.

The three document-wide streams now use same-origin WebSocket upgrades while retaining HTTP/UDS
SSE, redaction, cursor handling, fresh gateway tickets, and explicit shutdown. Web stop carries
captured session ownership, and its existing profile selector is declared in OpenAPI. Production
three-tab, stop/history, hidden-update, and reconnect walks verify commit `514de24d6`.

### CH-native-workspace-handler-boundary — Ada (hook and mutation-denial replay)

Two fresh hosted sessions use the same native memory proposal path under the configured project
hook. Operations attempts to save Studio's supplied release note; the hook executes once, attempts
a foreign workspace rewrite, and dispatch returns `hook_denied`. The approve-reads reviewer attempts
the supplied Editorial handoff directly. Its canonical Editorial permission request is rejected;
the actual write returns `workspace_access_denied`. Both agents report the unfinished work without
bypassing the refusal. Independent Studio and Editorial catalogs exactly match the before snapshots.
Public hook runs and session history retain one execution per session. Both sessions stop, the
owned hook is removed, and a fresh daemon confirms an empty pre-call catalog.

BUG-20261002-native-hook-dispatch-missing is verified in `e127956a3`. Red-before/green-after boot and duplicate
regressions, focused race suites, real hosted-MCP subprocess integration and the affected local gate
are retained in `native-hook-*.log`. Real-provider receipts are `native-hook-fixed-*`. The provider
ledger now contains ten live proof sessions; this count does not establish the broader collaboration
minimums. Row 6 remains Pending for the remaining allowed foreign-mutation and `all`-scope legs.
The initial cleanup request's unsupported `source` field was corrected from the live descriptor.

with status 400. Removing the filter recovers the project catalog. A fresh native read and the
direct task CLI retain the same three task IDs. This operator replay exercises shared dispatch;
no new hosted-session evidence is claimed. Receipts: `task-filter-*`. Bug
BUG-20261002-native-task-filter-error is verified; row 6 remains Pending for the hook and mutation legs.

## Paper Cuts

Long paths clipped the selected-project column and Skip button. BUG-20261002-onboarding-long-path-clipping is repaired in the working tree; fresh browser replay confirms both columns fit at four viewport widths. Browser zoom and RTL were not verified.

## Runtime Errors Observed

CLI discovery schema corruption, missing session JSONL provenance, and archived-profile fallback provenance are registered with their fixes. The intentionally stopped empty-lab daemon returned 502 errors that the onboarding surfaced and recovered from.

`/Users/pedronauck/.config/browser-harness/agent-workspace/recordings/compozy-untested-20261002`.

## Learnings

- The baseline contains 216 untested scenarios among 1,022 tracked scenarios (528 pass, 242 blocked-verify, 12 blocked-decision, 21 skipped, and 3 fail outside the baseline). Existing failures encountered on these journeys will be investigated.
- Generated state views and passing automated checks are not public-interface QA verdicts.

## Cross-Surface Impact

The owning audit is [Untested QA sweep — 2026-10-02](../../_memory/change-impact.md). The redactor repair changes public metadata classification without changing wire shapes, configuration, workspace state, or Web controls.

## Final Status

In progress. No completion, PR readiness, or release readiness claim. The latest affected local gate passes; remaining QA, current-head CI, evidence publication, and owned-process teardown remain pending.

## Commit-hook execution note

The first standard commit hook created and cleaned its automatic lint-staged backup before its
stash behavior was identified. Subsequent commits use a temporary copy of the same Husky hooks,
adding only `--no-stash --no-revert --no-hide-partially-staged`; all original formatting/lint checks
still run. No destructive Git command was issued directly, and unrelated work remains untouched.

### CH-native-workspace-handler-boundary — Ada (allowed Editorial handoff)

The fresh approve-all caller sess-5d81e1db831ad47c saves studio_coordination.md using the registered
name Editorial. Independent HTTP and fresh UDS reads return the supplied content from Editorial;
Studio's memory catalog remains unchanged. The useful reference is retained. Native task all scope
is refused with workspace_access_denied; an unbound operator's all-scope read succeeds.

The agent's natural project-by-project recovery exposes BUG-20261003-task-catalog-replaces-authorized-workspace:
Editorial name and path task queries return Studio's three tasks. Independent native HTTP and direct
CLI reads both return the correct empty Editorial catalog. The agent detects the mismatch rather
than presenting those rows as Editorial work. This is a failed catalog leg, not a failed provider
launch. The session is stopped, usage captured, and the walk ended before investigation. Evidence:
native-handoff-* in this cycle's directory. Row 6 remains Pending for repair/replay of this finding.

The two first descriptor lookups used the nonexistent tool describe verb with a workspace flag;
public help identified tool info and both descriptors were read successfully. These driver mistakes
are retained in evidence and are not classified as product defects.

### CH-profile-archive-race-guards — Dora (pending approval blocker)

Dora creates the owned, empty release-drafts profile and selects it in the isolated browser, then
invokes profile.delete through the documented CLI against that attached client. It returns one
approval_pending ID. Independent HTTP archive-plan and UDS delete-plan both name that ID; actual
archive and delete each refuse profile_approvals_pending. The profile retains work_items=0.

The owner-selected approvals show and cancel commands both return approval_not_found, including
show with the root profile flag preceding the subcommand. The pending blocker remains. Filed
BUG-20261003-approval-cli-profile-owner. The walk ends before source inspection, preserves the owned
profile/approval, and restores global CLI selection to studio. Browser recording is stopped at the
exact path in profile-race-ended.json. The isolated Chrome worked without a permission prompt.

Driver limitations are explicit: the guessed /api/approvals route is absent (404) and supplies no
approval-surface verdict; the first screenshot helper call had the wrong signature, so the visible
checkpoint was captured through standard CDP instead. Attention showed no unread notifications;
this observation alone is not a claim about the approval presentation contract. Namespace, queue,
spawn, extension-install and delivery-permit races have not run. Rows 11 and 12 remain Pending.
